<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-haiku-5-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Haiku 5.5</span><span class='zh'>Claude Haiku 5.5</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic released Claude Haiku 5.5, delivering improvements in reliability, safety, and efficiency for conversational AI.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Haiku 5.5，提升了对话式 AI 的可靠性、安全性与效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic released Claude Haiku 5.5, delivering improvements in reliability, safety, and efficiency for conversational AI. The update tightens safety guardrails, enhances instruction-following, and aims to deliver smoother multi-turn interactions.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Haiku 5.5，提升了对话式 AI 的可靠性、安全性与效率。更新强化了安全约束、改进了指令执行，并力求在多轮对话中提供更顺畅的体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New litigation accuses Visa, Mastercard, and major banks of anticompetitive merchant fees that raise costs for merchants and consumers. The case adds to regulatory scrutiny of card networks and could push fee structures toward reform.</span><span class='zh'>新诉讼指控 Visa、Mastercard 与多家银行联手设定反竞争的商户手续费，抬高商家和消费者成本。此案加剧监管对支付网络的关注，未来可能推动费率结构改革或和解结果。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08144" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Navier–Stokes Lost in Translation</span><span class='zh'>纳维-斯托克斯方程的翻译困境</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new paper examines how Navier–Stokes equations are translated into discrete algorithms and points out potential pitfalls that can lead to inaccurate or unstable simulations.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文探讨将 Navier–Stokes 方程转化为数值算法时可能出现的问题，揭示数值建模中的潜在误差与稳定性挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new paper examines how Navier–Stokes equations are translated into discrete algorithms and points out potential pitfalls that can lead to inaccurate or unstable simulations.</span><span class='zh'>一篇论文探讨将 Navier–Stokes 方程转化为数值算法时可能出现的问题，揭示数值建模中的潜在误差与稳定性挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project showcases animated ASCII art for websites, enabling lightweight, retro-styled visuals without images.</span><span class='zh'>该项目展示可在网页嵌入的动画 ASCII 艺术，提供轻量级、复古风格的可视效果与趣味 UI。</span></p>

The approach offers playful UI elements and can help reduce payloads for simple sites.</span><span class='zh'>该项目展示可在网页嵌入的动画 ASCII 艺术，提供轻量级、复古风格的可视效果与趣味 UI。对于追求极简前端或低带宽场景的实现者尤其友好。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://developer.chrome.com/blog/jpeg-xl-in-chrome" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shipping JPEG XL in Chrome</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 将原生支持 JPEG XL</span></a><span class="dom">developer.chrome.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chrome now ships native JPEG XL support, bringing next-gen image compression to the web. JPEG XL offers higher quality at smaller file sizes and supports features like animation and HDR, which could reduce bandwidth and streamline asset pipelines for publishers and developers.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome 已原生支持 JPEG XL，推动网页图像压缩进入新阶段。JPEG XL 在更小尺寸下提供更高画质，并支持动画与 HDR，有望降低带宽、简化资产工作流。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://openai.com/index/sharing-ai-progress-in-mathematics/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3430T8Aum4BAOFwLEnHQmC/bbc034e46bc5766dc6569183e87fa6df/ai-progress-in-mathematics-social.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/sharing-ai-progress-in-mathematics/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sharing AI progress in mathematics</span><span class='zh'>在数学领域分享人工智能进展</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI highlights progress in AI-assisted mathematics, including automated reasoning and conjecture generation.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 展示了 AI 在数学中的进展，包括自动推理与猜想生成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI highlights progress in AI-assisted mathematics, including automated reasoning and conjecture generation. The piece underscores collaboration with mathematicians and ongoing work to evaluate the reliability and safety of AI-assisted proofs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 展示了 AI 在数学中的进展，包括自动推理与猜想生成。文章强调与数学界的协作，以及在评估 AI 辅助证明的可靠性与安全性方面的持续努力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/szabadkai/c64-keyboard-font/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A font recreated from photographs of classic Commodore 64 keycaps</span><span class='zh'>从经典 Commodore 64 键帽照片重现的字体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design.</span><span class='zh'>一个字体完全由经典 Commodore 64 键帽的照片重建而成，展示了通过摄影测量和图像方法推动字体设计的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A font reconstructed entirely from photographs of Commodore 64 keycaps demonstrates how photogrammetry and image-based approaches can drive typography design. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/chemistry/2026/press-release/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobel Prize in Chemistry 2026 to Henri B. Kagan and Kenso Soai</span><span class='zh'>2026 年诺贝尔化学奖授予 Henri B. Kagan 与 Kenso Soai</span></a><span class="dom">nobelprize.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The 2026 Nobel Prize in Chemistry goes to Henri B.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年诺贝尔化学奖授予 Henri B. Kagan 与 Kenso Soai，表彰其在不对称合成与催化方面的开创性工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The 2026 Nobel Prize in Chemistry goes to Henri B. Kagan and Kenso Soai for foundational work in asymmetric synthesis and catalysis. Their breakthroughs enable more selective creation of chiral molecules, with implications for pharmaceuticals, materials, and fundamental chemistry.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年诺贝尔化学奖授予 Henri B. Kagan 与 Kenso Soai，表彰其在不对称合成与催化方面的开创性工作。其突破有助于更高选择性地制造手性分子，影响药物、材料以及基础化学研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://strandsagents.com/blog/introducing-strands-decider/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Strands Decider 2B: a small, open-source, decision model</span><span class='zh'>Strands Decider 2B：一个小巧的开源决策模型</span></a><span class="dom">strandsagents.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Strands Decider 2B is a compact, open-source decision model designed for lightweight agent architectures.</span><span class='zh'>Strands Decider 2B 是为轻量级智能体架构设计的紧凑开源决策模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Strands Decider 2B is a compact, open-source decision model designed for lightweight agent architectures. It presents a modular, pluggable decision-making framework that lets researchers experiment with different strategies without heavy infrastructure. The takeaway is that it lowers barriers to building transparent, auditable AI agents and accelerates prototyping of decision policies.</span><span class='zh'>Strands Decider 2B 是为轻量级智能体架构设计的紧凑开源决策模型。它提供模块化、可插拔的决策框架，便于研究者在不依赖繁重基础设施的情况下试验不同策略。其意义在于降低构建可解释、可审计的智能体决策系统的门槛，加速决策策略的原型开发。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer posts about GitHub failing to remove cracked copies of their software despite takedown requests, highlighting enforcement gaps and the friction between licensing, piracy, and platform moderation. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/boykopovar/AnyPS5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AnyPS5: Port PS5 binaries to PC without emulation (87% system libraries mapped)</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5：在不使用模拟的情况下将 PS5 二进制移植到 PC（87% 系统库已映射）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AnyPS5 maps the majority of PS5 system libraries to PC, enabling native execution of PS5 binaries on x86 hardware without full emulation. The project showcases progress in cross-architecture porting but raises legal, licensing, and anti-tampering questions as it matures.</span><span class='zh'>AnyPS5 将 PS5 系统库大部分映射到 PC，从而在 x86 硬件上本地运行 PS5 二进制，而无需完整仿真。该项目展示了跨架构移植的进展，但随着成熟，也引发法律、授权和反篡改等方面的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/decisions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decisions API is in public beta</span><span class='zh'>Decisions API 已进入公开测试版</span></a><span class="dom">developers.openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decisions API is in public beta, enabling developers to encode multi-step decision making into apps using the model’s planning and recommendation capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Decisions API 现处公开测试版，允许开发者在应用中嵌入多步决策能力，利用模型的规划与推荐功能输出选项、权衡与建议行动，并可对结果设定约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decisions API is in public beta, enabling developers to encode multi-step decision making into apps using the model’s planning and recommendation capabilities. It helps build tools that surface options, trade-offs, and recommended actions with controllable constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Decisions API 现处公开测试版，允许开发者在应用中嵌入多步决策能力，利用模型的规划与推荐功能输出选项、权衡与建议行动，并可对结果设定约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mistral Large 4: &quot;Le Chonk&quot;</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4：Le Chonk</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral Large 4 Le Chonk is a variant of the Mistral Large 4 release, showing tweaks that optimize performance for specific workloads.</span><span class='zh'>对 Mistral Large 4 的 Le Chonk 变体进行解读，揭示其在任务定制或领域适应方面的性能调整。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral Large 4 Le Chonk is a variant of the Mistral Large 4 release, showing tweaks that optimize performance for specific workloads. The coverage signals the model family’s expansion and ongoing experimentation in model variants.</span><span class='zh'>对 Mistral Large 4 的 Le Chonk 变体进行解读，揭示其在任务定制或领域适应方面的性能调整。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/embeddinggemma-2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2: An open, lightweight multimodal embedding model</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2：一个开源、轻量级的多模态嵌入模型</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2 builds on Google&#x27;s open Gemma line with a lightweight, multimodal embedding model designed for fast retrieval across text and vision.</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 在开放的 Gemma 基础上，提供一个轻量级的多模态嵌入模型，面向快速跨模态检索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EmbeddingGemma 2 builds on Google&#x27;s open Gemma line with a lightweight, multimodal embedding model designed for fast retrieval across text and vision. By staying open and small, it lowers barriers for researchers and developers to prototype cross-modal apps and run experiments on modest hardware.</span><span class='zh'>EmbeddingGemma 2 在开放的 Gemma 基础上，提供一个轻量级的多模态嵌入模型，面向快速跨模态检索。保持开源和体积小，降低研究者和开发者在中等硬件上原型化跨模态应用的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nobelprize.org/prizes/physics/2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobel Prize in Physics 2026: Francis Halzen</span><span class='zh'>2026年诺贝尔物理学奖：弗朗西斯·哈尔岑</span></a><span class="dom">nobelprize.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Francis Halzen is named a Nobel laureate for physics this year, recognized for his pioneering work in neutrino astronomy and the IceCube detector. His contributions helped establish high-energy neutrinos as a major new window on the cosmos, enabling discoveries about distant cosmic sources.</span><span class='zh'>弗朗西斯·哈尔岑因在中微子天文学方面的开创性工作及冰立方探测器的贡献获诺贝尔物理学奖。他的工作把高能中微子确立为研究宇宙的新窗口，推动了对遥远宇宙源头的理解。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Paramount Skydance completed its $111B merger with Warner Bros.</span><span class='zh'>派拉蒙-斯凯丹斯完成对华纳兄弟探索公司1110亿美元合并，形成覆盖大片 IP、工作室与流媒体的庞大整合体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Paramount Skydance completed its $111B merger with Warner Bros. Discovery, creating a media behemoth with scale across studios, franchises, and streaming. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FeSens/openTPU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenTPU – An open-source AI accelerator, developed by AI</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU：一个开源的AI加速器项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenTPU is an open-source AI accelerator project offering hardware design and software for researchers to explore acceleration architectures. By inviting community contributions, it aims to democratize access to AI hardware and accelerate experimentation beyond vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenTPU 是一个开源的 AI 加速器项目，提供硬件设计和软件栈，便于研究者探索不同的加速架构。通过鼓励社区贡献，它旨在降低对厂商锁定的依赖，推动 AI 硬件的普及与创新。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techdirt.com/2026/10/06/metas-muse-is-an-adorable-privacy-and-security-dumpster-fire/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta’s Muse is an adorable privacy and security dumpster fire</span><span class='zh'>Meta 的 Muse：一个关于隐私与安全的争议平台</span></a><span class="dom">techdirt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s Muse is criticized for a privacy and security dumpster fire, with critics arguing the assistant mishandles data and raises risk.</span><span class='zh'>关于 Muse 的隐私与安全问题引发批评，质疑这类 AI 助手在数据处理上的风险与保护不足。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s Muse is criticized for a privacy and security dumpster fire, with critics arguing the assistant mishandles data and raises risk. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbc.ca/radio/asithappens/milwaukee-swatting-couple-9.7370118" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Couple was swatted 55 times in 2 years over a post about Norm Macdonald</span><span class='zh'>一对夫妻因一则关于 Norm Macdonald 的帖子，在两年内遭遇了 55 次 SWATTING（恶作剧式警察骚扰）</span></a><span class="dom">cbc.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Milwaukee couple endured 55 swatting incidents over two years triggered by a single online post about Norm Macdonald, illustrating serious real-world risks from online harassment and doxxing.</span><span class='zh'>一对来自密尔沃基的夫妻因一则关于 Norm Macdonald 的帖子，在两年内遭遇了 55 次 SWATTING（恶作剧式警察骚扰）。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Milwaukee couple endured 55 swatting incidents over two years triggered by a single online post about Norm Macdonald, illustrating serious real-world risks from online harassment and doxxing.</span><span class='zh'>一对来自密尔沃基的夫妻因一则关于 Norm Macdonald 的帖子，在两年内遭遇了 55 次 SWATTING（恶作剧式警察骚扰）。此事凸显网络骚扰的现实危害，以及保护受害者免受此类攻击的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phys.org/news/2026-10-nature-capacity-species-lost-vastly.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nature&#x27;s capacity to &#x27;bounce back&#x27; when species are lost is overestimated: study</span><span class='zh'>物种灭绝后自然界的恢复能力被严重高估</span></a><span class="dom">phys.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New research shows nature&#x27;s capacity to recover after species loss is vastly overestimated; simulations reveal that ecosystems may undergo slow recovery or irreversible shifts, with cascading effects.</span><span class='zh'>新研究表明，自然界在物种流失后恢复的能力被严重高估；模拟显示生态系统可能缓慢恢复或发生不可逆转的转变，产生连锁效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New research shows nature&#x27;s capacity to recover after species loss is vastly overestimated; simulations reveal that ecosystems may undergo slow recovery or irreversible shifts, with cascading effects. The finding implies conservation strategies should prioritize preventing losses of keystone species and maintaining network robustness.</span><span class='zh'>新研究表明，自然界在物种流失后恢复的能力被严重高估；模拟显示生态系统可能缓慢恢复或发生不可逆转的转变，产生连锁效应。该发现提醒在保护策略中应优先防止关键物种流失，维护生态网络的韧性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/chatgpt-is-adding-real-cartoonists-signatures-to-fake-new-yorker-cartoons/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ChatGPT is adding real cartoonists&#x27; signatures to fake New Yorker cartoons</span><span class='zh'>ChatGPT 将真实漫画家的签名添加到假冒的《纽约客》漫画中</span></a><span class="dom">niemanlab.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-generated cartoons are now reported to carry signatures from real cartoonists, raising intellectual property and attribution concerns as authorship lines blur. This development underscores the challenges of provenance in AI-generated art and the potential need for watermarking or policy safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的漫画如今被指会带上真实漫画家的签名，这模糊了署名与版权的边界，凸显了 AI 生成作品在来源认定上的挑战以及对水印与政策保护的潜在需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://reflection.ai/blog/introducing-beam" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Beam: Reflection&#x27;s 501B open-weight model</span><span class='zh'>Beam：Reflection 的 501B 开放权重模型发布</span></a><span class="dom">reflection.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways.</span><span class='zh'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reflection AI rolls out Beam 501B as an open-weight model, allowing researchers to run a large language model locally without gateways. This expands access and invites community evaluation, with emphasis on safety and alignment considerations.</span><span class='zh'>Reflection AI 发布 Beam 501B 开放权重模型，允许研究者在本地运行大型语言模型，降低对远端服务的依赖。开放权重将推动研究者协同评估，并强调安全与对齐的重要性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://gleam.run/news/gleam-doesnt-compile-to-erlang-source-anymore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://gleam.run/images/preview/v1.19.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='gleam.run' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gleam.run/news/gleam-doesnt-compile-to-erlang-source-anymore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gleam doesn&#x27;t compile to Erlang source anymore</span><span class='zh'>Gleam 不再直接编译为 Erlang 源码</span></a><span class="dom">gleam.run</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Gleam compiler will no longer emit Erlang source code; it now targets BEAM bytecode directly.</span><span class='zh'>Gleam 编译器不再输出 Erlang 源码，而改为直接编译成 BEAM 字节码，减少对 Erlang 源码生成的依赖，简化工具链，并提升与 Elixir/Erlang 项目的互操作性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Gleam compiler will no longer emit Erlang source code; it now targets BEAM bytecode directly. This reduces dependency on Erlang source generation and streamlines toolchains, potentially improving interoperability with Elixir and Erlang projects, though it may alter existing code-generation workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Gleam 编译器不再输出 Erlang 源码，而改为直接编译成 BEAM 字节码，减少对 Erlang 源码生成的依赖，简化工具链，并提升与 Elixir/Erlang 项目的互操作性。此举可能影响现有的代码生成工作流。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>313</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49975619' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>135</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49970667" data-id="49970667" data-tags="AI|Science|Hardware" data-score="488" data-comments="333" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49970667" data-time="1791234021">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.vals.ai/blogs/room-temperature-magnetic-semiconductors" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Opus 5.5 agents discover two room-temperature magnetic semiconductor candidates</span><span class='zh'>Opus 5.5 的代理发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料</span></a><span class="dom">vals.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents in the Opus framework autonomously discovered two candidate room-temperature magnetic semiconductors, pushing spintronics closer to practical devices.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 框架中的 AI 代理自动发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料，为自旋电子学向实用器件推进带来希望。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents in the Opus framework autonomously discovered two candidate room-temperature magnetic semiconductors, pushing spintronics closer to practical devices. This showcases how AI-assisted materials discovery can accelerate breakthroughs that once seemed out of reach.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 框架中的 AI 代理自动发现两种室温磁性半导体候选材料，为自旋电子学向实用器件推进带来希望。这体现了 AI 辅助的材料发现能显著加速原本难以实现的突破。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://citizenlab.ca/toronto-based-vpn-provider-plans-to-quit-canada-over-lawful-access-bill/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Toronto-Based VPN Provider Plans to Quit Canada over Lawful-Access Bill</span><span class='zh'>多伦多 VPN 服务商计划因法定访问法案退出加拿大</span></a><span class="dom">citizenlab.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Toronto-based VPN provider plans to quit Canada over a lawful-access bill, citing compliance burdens and potential data-access requirements.</span><span class='zh'>一家多伦多 VPN 提供商计划因法定访问法案退出加拿大，指称合规负担与潜在数据访问要求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Toronto-based VPN provider plans to quit Canada over a lawful-access bill, citing compliance burdens and potential data-access requirements. The move underscores the friction between user privacy protections and government surveillance laws, with implications for Canadian users and the VPN market.</span><span class='zh'>一家多伦多 VPN 提供商计划因法定访问法案退出加拿大，指称合规负担与潜在数据访问要求。此举凸显用户隐私保护与政府监控法规之间的冲突，对加拿大用户与 VPN 市场产生影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vivien Henz argues that Common Lisp&#x27;s macro system, homoiconicity, and minimal runtime yield unrivaled metaprogramming and rapid DSL creation.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为 Common Lisp 的宏机制、同构性和极小的运行时开销，使其在元编程和快速构建领域无出其右。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/01/worlds-first-enhanced-geothermal-power-plant-completed-in-just-23-months/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>First enhanced geothermal power plant completed in just 23 months</span><span class='zh'>全球首座增强型地热电站仅用23个月完工</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The world&#x27;s first enhanced geothermal power plant was completed in 23 months, setting a record pace for rapid deployment of Enhanced Geothermal Systems (EGS).</span><span class='zh'>全球首座增强型地热电站用时仅23个月完工，刷新相关项目的施工速度记录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The world&#x27;s first enhanced geothermal power plant was completed in 23 months, setting a record pace for rapid deployment of Enhanced Geothermal Systems (EGS). The milestone demonstrates the potential to scale baseload clean energy with faster deployments and reduced risk, signaling a path toward broader geothermal adoption.</span><span class='zh'>全球首座增强型地热电站用时仅23个月完工，刷新相关项目的施工速度记录。该成就证明通过增强地热系统可以实现更快部署与风险降低，从而扩大稳定清洁能源的潜力，推动地热在全球的更广泛应用。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/10/6/codemode/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-10-06-codemode-social.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='lucumr.pocoo.org' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">43</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/10/6/codemode/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What is Codemode</span><span class='zh'>Codemode 是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">lucumr.pocoo.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codemode introduces a way to think about code operation modes as first class concepts.</span><span class='zh'>Codemode 将代码在不同运行环境中的行为视为一等公民，开发、生产、测试等模式成为设计的核心概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codemode introduces a way to think about code operation modes as first class concepts. The piece argues for structured patterns that let software adapt behavior across environments with predictable outcomes, improving reliability and security.</span><span class='zh'>Codemode 将代码在不同运行环境中的行为视为一等公民，开发、生产、测试等模式成为设计的核心概念。通过提出结构化模式，帮助代码在不同环境下自适应、可预测，从而提升可靠性与安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://qlabs.sh/research/dust" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dust: Pretraining Transformers Without Backpropagation</span><span class='zh'>Dust：无需反向传播的 Transformer 预训练</span></a><span class="dom">qlabs.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dust introduces a pretraining approach for Transformers that does not rely on backpropagation, exploring alternative optimization or local-target strategies.</span><span class='zh'>Dust 提出一种在不依赖反向传播的情况下对 Transformer 进行预训练的方法，探索替代优化或局部目标策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dust introduces a pretraining approach for Transformers that does not rely on backpropagation, exploring alternative optimization or local-target strategies. Early results suggest comparable performance with different training dynamics and potential efficiency gains.</span><span class='zh'>Dust 提出一种在不依赖反向传播的情况下对 Transformer 进行预训练的方法，探索替代优化或局部目标策略。初步结果显示在训练动力学与效率方面可能带来不同的优势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ersc.io/blog/when-random-isnt-random-enough" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When random is not actually random enough</span><span class='zh'>当随机性并不真正足够随机</span></a><span class="dom">ersc.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores gaps between theoretical randomness and real RNGs, highlighting biases in hardware and software entropy sources and showing that well-seeded generators can still fail statistical tests.</span><span class='zh'>揭示理论上的随机性与实际随机源之间的差距，强调硬件与软件熵源可能存在偏见，即使经过精心种子也可能在统计测试中失效。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores gaps between theoretical randomness and real RNGs, highlighting biases in hardware and software entropy sources and showing that well-seeded generators can still fail statistical tests.</span><span class='zh'>揭示理论上的随机性与实际随机源之间的差距，强调硬件与软件熵源可能存在偏见，即使经过精心种子也可能在统计测试中失效。此类问题对密码学和机器学习工作负载具有重要影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare released a Web Search API that lets developers integrate search capabilities into apps with Cloudflare&#x27;s indexing, safety filters, and ranking.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 公布了网页搜索 API，开发者可以在应用中集成搜索功能，利用 Cloudflare 的索引、安全过滤和排序。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Presents subquadratic-time results for the 3SUM problem and subcubic-time results for all-pairs shortest path under standard complexity conjectures, pushing forward the frontier of combinatorial optimization.</span><span class='zh'>给出在常见复杂性假设下的 3SUM 子二次时间及 APSP 子立方时间的算法进展，推动组合优化领域的前沿。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Presents subquadratic-time results for the 3SUM problem and subcubic-time results for all-pairs shortest path under standard complexity conjectures, pushing forward the frontier of combinatorial optimization.</span><span class='zh'>给出在常见复杂性假设下的 3SUM 子二次时间及 APSP 子立方时间的算法进展，推动组合优化领域的前沿。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large Danish data breach exposed personal data for about 8.8 million people due to unauthorized access.</span><span class='zh'>未经授权的访问导致约880万丹麦公民的个人信息被曝光（包括 CPR 编号等）。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large Danish data breach exposed personal data for about 8.8 million people due to unauthorized access. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://cedardb.com/blog/sqldoom/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We ported the original Doom to SQL</span><span class='zh'>我们将原版 Doom 移植到 SQL</span></a><span class="dom">cedardb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful project porting the original Doom to SQL showcases expressing game logic within a relational database.</span><span class='zh'>这个有趣的项目把原版 Doom 移植到 SQL，展示在关系型数据库中实现游戏逻辑的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful project porting the original Doom to SQL showcases expressing game logic within a relational database. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://nivdayan.github.io/dostoevsky.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Better Space-Time Trade-Offs for LSM-Tree Based Key-Value Stores [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>针对基于 LSM-树的键值存储的更优空间-时间权衡 [PDF]</span></a><span class="dom">nivdayan.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This paper presents improved space-time trade-offs for log-structured merge-tree (LSM-tree) based KV stores, with implications for throughput, write amplification, and storage density.</span><span class='zh'>论文提出改进的基于 LSM-树的键值存储在空间和时间上的权衡，对吞吐、写放大与存储密度等指标有影响，将为 RocksDB、LevelDB 等数据库的合并策略带来优化空间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This paper presents improved space-time trade-offs for log-structured merge-tree (LSM-tree) based KV stores, with implications for throughput, write amplification, and storage density. The result can influence database systems like RocksDB and LevelDB by optimizing compaction strategies.</span><span class='zh'>论文提出改进的基于 LSM-树的键值存储在空间和时间上的权衡，对吞吐、写放大与存储密度等指标有影响，将为 RocksDB、LevelDB 等数据库的合并策略带来优化空间。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.osna.fm/german-military-intelligence-uses-ai-to-screen-bundeswehr-applicants-for-right-wing-extremism/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>German Bundeswehr Uses AI to Screen Applicants for Right-Wing Extremism</span><span class='zh'>德國聯邦國防軍使用 AI 篩查應徵者的右翼極端主義傾向</span></a><span class="dom">news.osna.fm</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Germany&#x27;s federal armed forces are reportedly deploying AI to screen job applicants for signals of right-wing extremism.</span><span class='zh'>據報告，德國聯邦國防軍開始用 AI 篩查應徵者中與右翼極端主義相關的信號。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Germany&#x27;s federal armed forces are reportedly deploying AI to screen job applicants for signals of right-wing extremism. While this speeds up risk-based screening, it raises questions about bias, due process, and civil-liberties safeguards in automated vetting of recruits. The move highlights how militaries are piloting ML in personnel decisions, with potential spillovers to other safety-critical hiring.</span><span class='zh'>據報告，德國聯邦國防軍開始用 AI 篩查應徵者中與右翼極端主義相關的信號。此舉提升了篩檢效率，但也引發偏見、程序正當性和公民自由等方面的質疑——自動審核的人員招募日益成為實驗場。此案例反映出軍隊在人才招募中測試機器學習的趨勢及其對其他領域的影響。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06783" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Subquadratic 3SUM and Subcubic APSP</span><span class='zh'>子平方级3SUM与子三次APSP的新算法</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Theoretical computer scientists present algorithms achieving subquadratic time for 3SUM and subcubic time for APSP, narrowing gaps in fundamental computational problems.</span><span class='zh'>理论计算机科学家提出对3SUM实现子平方时间、对APSP实现子三次时间的算法，推动这两类基础问题时间界限的突破。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Theoretical computer scientists present algorithms achieving subquadratic time for 3SUM and subcubic time for APSP, narrowing gaps in fundamental computational problems. These results may influence practical databases, graph algorithms, and complexity theory research.</span><span class='zh'>理论计算机科学家提出对3SUM实现子平方时间、对APSP实现子三次时间的算法，推动这两类基础问题时间界限的突破。相关结果可能影响数据库、图算法以及复杂度理论的研究与应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://restofworld.org/2026/ai-data-center-memory-chip-shortage-cheap-smartphones-digital-divide/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The AI boom is making the cheapest smartphones disappear</span><span class='zh'>AI 热潮正在让最便宜的智能手机消失</span></a><span class="dom">restofworld.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI boom and memory-chip shortages at data centers are driving up costs and reducing the availability of ultra-cheap smartphones, widening the digital divide in emerging markets.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能热潮及数据中心内存芯片短缺推高成本，导致极便宜的智能手机供应受限，进一步拉大新兴市场的数字鸿沟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI boom and memory-chip shortages at data centers are driving up costs and reducing the availability of ultra-cheap smartphones, widening the digital divide in emerging markets. The piece links hardware constraints to consumer access and growth trajectories.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能热潮及数据中心内存芯片短缺推高成本，导致极便宜的智能手机供应受限，进一步拉大新兴市场的数字鸿沟。文章将硬件约束与你的可及性和移动增长前景联系起来。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danielchasehooper.com/posts/typechecked-generic-c-data-structures/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type Safe Generic Data Structures in C (2025)</span><span class='zh'>C 语言中的类型安全通用数据结构</span></a><span class="dom">danielchasehooper.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece explores building type-safe generic data structures in C, likely using macros, _Generic, or tagging techniques to avoid void* pitfalls.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨在 C 语言中构建类型安全的通用数据结构，可能借助宏、_Generic 或标签等技术，避免 void* 的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece explores building type-safe generic data structures in C, likely using macros, _Generic, or tagging techniques to avoid void* pitfalls. It shows how to achieve more robust and maintainable generic abstractions in a language without built-in generics.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨在 C 语言中构建类型安全的通用数据结构，可能借助宏、_Generic 或标签等技术，避免 void* 的风险。展示了在无内建泛型的语言中实现更健壮、可维护的通用抽象的方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.citp.princeton.edu/2026/08/03/an-algorithmic-failure-beneath-the-secret-ballot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An algorithmic failure beneath the secret ballot</span><span class='zh'>秘密投票背后的算法失灵</span></a><span class="dom">blog.citp.princeton.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A look at how algorithmic processes in elections can fail under the hood, from ballot handling to cryptographic verification.</span><span class='zh'>剖析选举背后的算法层面可能出现的失效，从选票处理到加密校验，指出若干可能破坏选票保密性、选民可验证性与公众信任的故障模式，并提出加强审计的思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A look at how algorithmic processes in elections can fail under the hood, from ballot handling to cryptographic verification.</span><span class='zh'>剖析选举背后的算法层面可能出现的失效，从选票处理到加密校验，指出若干可能破坏选票保密性、选民可验证性与公众信任的故障模式，并提出加强审计的思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://liquidbrain.net/blog/accountability-and-joy/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Accountability mechanisms can be joyful (2024)</span><span class='zh'>問責機制亦可帶來快樂</span></a><span class="dom">liquidbrain.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Designers are exploring accountability mechanisms that feel constructive rather than punitive.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张问责机制不必只带来压力，应通过清晰的反馈、包容的治理和人性化激励，让参与者愿意承担责任而非抵触。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Designers are exploring accountability mechanisms that feel constructive rather than punitive. The piece argues that clear feedback loops, inclusive governance, and humane incentives can make accountability something people buy into, not dread. When done right, accountability can improve trust and performance without eroding autonomy.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张问责机制不必只带来压力，应通过清晰的反馈、包容的治理和人性化激励，让参与者愿意承担责任而非抵触。若设计得当，问责可以提升信任和绩效，同时保护自主性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blue-continuum.com/the-man-who-listens-to-whales" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using A.I. to decode communication of sperm whales</span><span class='zh'>用 AI 解码座头鲸的沟通</span></a><span class="dom">blue-continuum.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using AI to decode sperm whale communication, researchers are analyzing patterns and context to infer potential meanings behind whale vocalizations.</span><span class='zh'>研究团队利用 AI 解码座头鲸的沟通，分析模式与语境，尝试推断鲸声背后的潜在含义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using AI to decode sperm whale communication, researchers are analyzing patterns and context to infer potential meanings behind whale vocalizations. The work illustrates how machine learning can augment marine biology, uncovering insights into animal communication at scale.</span><span class='zh'>研究团队利用 AI 解码座头鲸的沟通，分析模式与语境，尝试推断鲸声背后的潜在含义。该工作展示了机器学习在海洋生物学中的应用，能够大规模揭示动物沟通的规律。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dryperspective.github.io/posts/complement-of-true/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The complement of true is true, except when it&#x27;s false</span><span class='zh'>真值的补集：总为真，除非它为假</span></a><span class="dom">dryperspective.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece delves into logical truth values and complement operations, presenting a provocative angle on how negation interacts with truth.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨逻辑真值及其补集运算，提出对取非与真值关系的有趣观察，揭示在形式语义、类型系统和逻辑编程中影响深远的边界情形。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece delves into logical truth values and complement operations, presenting a provocative angle on how negation interacts with truth. It ties into formal semantics and highlights surprising edge cases that matter for verification, type systems, and logic programming.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨逻辑真值及其补集运算，提出对取非与真值关系的有趣观察，揭示在形式语义、类型系统和逻辑编程中影响深远的边界情形。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.haskell.org/foldl-and-foldr/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Differences Between `Foldl` and `Foldr`</span><span class='zh'>Foldl 与 Foldr 的区别</span></a><span class="dom">blog.haskell.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Foldl and Foldr are core Haskell folds with different performance characteristics.</span><span class='zh'>Foldl 与 Foldr 是 Haskell 中的两种折叠操作，有不同的性能特性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Foldl and Foldr are core Haskell folds with different performance characteristics. Foldr can work with infinite lists and is lazy, while Foldl (especially without strictness) can cause space leaks; choosing between them depends on data structure and evaluation strategy.</span><span class='zh'>Foldl 与 Foldr 是 Haskell 中的两种折叠操作，有不同的性能特性。Foldr 能处理无限列表且具备惰性求值，而 Foldl（若不使用严格化）可能导致空间泄漏；选择取决于数据结构和求值策略。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">117</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blogs.gnome.org/mcatanzaro/2026/10/02/the-era-of-software-quality-or-the-era-of-ostriches/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The era of software quality, or the era of ostriches?</span><span class='zh'>软件质量的时代，还是鸵鸟式时代？</span></a><span class="dom">blogs.gnome.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative take on whether the software industry prioritizes speed over quality, urging practices to avoid ‘ostrich’ behavior and improve software dependability.</span><span class='zh'>对软件行业是否把速度置于质量之上提出质疑，呼吁避免“鸵鸟式”态度并提升软件可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative take on whether the software industry prioritizes speed over quality, urging practices to avoid ‘ostrich’ behavior and improve software dependability.</span><span class='zh'>对软件行业是否把速度置于质量之上提出质疑，呼吁避免“鸵鸟式”态度并提升软件可靠性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.stelabouras.com/blog/entombed-raycaster/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Entombed in a Raycaster</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：被埋在光线投射器中的实体</span></a><span class="dom">stelabouras.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Entombed in a Raycaster showcases a compact raycasting project, likely exploring 2D rendering with a retro aesthetic.</span><span class='zh'>《Entombed in a Raycaster》展示了一个紧凑的光线投射项目，以复古美感探索二维渲染。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Entombed in a Raycaster showcases a compact raycasting project, likely exploring 2D rendering with a retro aesthetic. It offers an accessible entry point to game-dev techniques, with practical lessons on raycasting performance and simplicity.</span><span class='zh'>《Entombed in a Raycaster》展示了一个紧凑的光线投射项目，以复古美感探索二维渲染。它为游戏开发初学者提供了实用的实现要点，涉及光线投射的性能与简洁性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iopscience.iop.org/article/10.1088/1361-6404/aea271" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using Theme park rides and smartphones for demonstrating Newtonian mechanics</span><span class='zh'>通过主题乐园的设施和智能手机进行牛顿力学的课堂演示</span></a><span class="dom">iopscience.iop.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on teaching approach uses roller coasters and smartphones to demonstrate Newton’s laws, including data capture via sensors and intuitive demonstrations of inertia and acceleration.</span><span class='zh'>通过主题乐园的设施和智能手机进行牛顿力学的课堂演示，涵盖数据采集与对惯性、加速度等概念的直观体现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on teaching approach uses roller coasters and smartphones to demonstrate Newton’s laws, including data capture via sensors and intuitive demonstrations of inertia and acceleration.</span><span class='zh'>通过主题乐园的设施和智能手机进行牛顿力学的课堂演示，涵盖数据采集与对惯性、加速度等概念的直观体现。文章介绍了兼具趣味性和学术性的教学实验设计。</span></p>
